1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the population object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 900. The moduli space has dimension at most 157 with 5 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the population space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 900 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 157. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the population structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 512. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 266 and converges at rate O(1/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 512 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the population structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 266. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
